========== PLIK: tests\gesture_samples.py ==========
"""Synthetic geometry, not recordings or evidence of real-world accuracy."""

from dataclasses import replace

from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.definitions import Gesture
from src.gesture_assistant.vision.hand_provider import Hand, HandPoint, HandResult


def sample(gesture=Gesture.OPEN_PALM, side="Right", dx=0.0, dy=0.0, scale=1.0):
    p = [(0.5, 0.85, 0.0)] * 21
    fingers = ((5, 0.38, 0.62), (9, 0.5, 0.6), (13, 0.59, 0.62), (17, 0.69, 0.65))
    extended = {
        Gesture.OPEN_PALM: (True, True, True, True),
        Gesture.FIST: (False, False, False, False),
        Gesture.THUMBS_UP: (False, False, False, False),
        Gesture.THUMBS_DOWN: (False, False, False, False),
        Gesture.VICTORY: (True, True, False, False),
        Gesture.POINTING_UP: (True, False, False, False),
        Gesture.PINCH: (True, True, True, True),
        Gesture.ROCK: (False, False, False, True),
        Gesture.UNKNOWN: (True, False, True, False),
    }[gesture]
    for (index, x, y), is_extended in zip(fingers, extended, strict=True):
        if is_extended:
            coords = [(x, y), (x, y - 0.10), (x, y - 0.17), (x, y - 0.25)]
        else:
            coords = [(x, y), (x, y - 0.10), (x + 0.02, y + 0.03), (x + 0.01, y + 0.08)]
        p[index : index + 4] = [(a, b, 0.0) for a, b in coords]
    thumb = [(0.43, 0.76), (0.44, 0.70), (0.50, 0.72), (0.53, 0.74)]
    if gesture in (Gesture.OPEN_PALM, Gesture.ROCK):
        thumb = [(0.43, 0.76), (0.34, 0.70), (0.25, 0.65), (0.16, 0.60)]
    if gesture == Gesture.THUMBS_UP:
        thumb = [(0.42, 0.76), (0.38, 0.70), (0.38, 0.58), (0.38, 0.46)]
    if gesture == Gesture.THUMBS_DOWN:
        thumb = [(0.42, 0.70), (0.35, 0.73), (0.35, 0.85), (0.35, 0.98)]
    if gesture == Gesture.PINCH:
        thumb = [(0.43, 0.76), (0.32, 0.63), (0.30, 0.48), (0.38, 0.38)]
    p[1:5] = [(a, b, 0.0) for a, b in thumb]
    points = []
    for x, y, z in p:
        if side == "Left":
            x = 1 - x
        points.append(HandPoint((x - 0.5) * scale + 0.5 + dx, (y - 0.5) * scale + 0.5 + dy, z))
    return Hand(tuple(points), (), side, 0.99)


def frame(hand, timestamp_ms):
    return HandResult(timestamp_ms + 1, timestamp_ms, () if hand is None else (hand,))


def low_score(hand):
    return replace(hand, handedness_score=0.2)

from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.keyboard_actions import validate_shortcut
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.native_backend import require_guard


class FakeKeyboard:
    """Records validated requests, never calls SendInput or any OS function."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = []

    def hotkey(self, value, guard, allow_reserved=False):
        keys = validate_shortcut(value, allow_reserved)
        require_guard(guard)
        self.calls.append((value, keys, allow_reserved))

========== PLIK: tests\test_action_executor.py ==========
import threading

from src.gesture_assistant.core.action_executor import ActionExecutor
from src.gesture_assistant.core.action_runtime import ActionRuntime
from tests.action_samples import Clock, analysis, context, policy, ready


def test_only_injected_fake_handler_executes():
    safety, clock, detector = ready()
    request = safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect())
    calls = []
    handlers = {"teams.toggle_mute": lambda: calls.append(1)}
    executor = ActionExecutor(safety, detector, handlers, False)
    outcome = executor.execute(request)
    assert outcome.executed and calls == [1]
    assert not executor.execute(request).executed


def test_two_executions_never_overlap():
    safety, clock, detector = ready()
    request = safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect())
    entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
    outcomes = []

    def handler():
        entered.set()
        assert release.wait(3), "Test nie zwolnił handlera."

    executor = ActionExecutor(safety, detector, {"teams.toggle_mute": handler}, False)
    thread = threading.Thread(target=lambda: outcomes.append(executor.execute(request)))
    try:
        thread.start()
        assert entered.wait(3)
        # Even a second dispatch of the request must not enter the handler.
        assert executor.execute(request).reason == "busy"
    finally:
        release.set()
        thread.join(timeout=3)
    assert not thread.is_alive()
    assert len(outcomes) == 1 and outcomes[0].executed


def test_failed_handler_has_cooldown_and_does_not_leave_busy_flag():
    safety, clock, detector = ready()
    request = safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect())

    def fail():
        raise OSError("fake failure")

    outcome = ActionExecutor(safety, detector, {"teams.toggle_mute": fail}, False).execute(request)
    assert not outcome.executed and "handler_error" in outcome.reason
    assert safety._active is None
    assert safety._action_until == clock() + 2000


def test_context_changed_between_two_checks_blocks_fake_handler():
    safety, clock, detector = ready()
    request = safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect())
    calls = []
    original_detect = detector.detect

    def changed(mode):
        result = original_detect(mode)
        detector.reader = context(clock, "chrome.exe").reader
        return result

    detector.detect = changed
    handlers = {"teams.toggle_mute": lambda: calls.append(1)}
    outcome = ActionExecutor(safety, detector, handlers, False)
    assert outcome.execute(request).reason == "authorization_changed"
    assert not calls


def test_runtime_uses_continuous_observation_not_early_event():
    clock = Clock()
    config = policy()
    config["bindings"] = {"FIST": "teams.toggle_mute"}
    runtime = ActionRuntime(config, context(clock), clock)
    for stamp in range(0, 701, 100):
        clock.value = stamp
        runtime.observe(analysis(stamp))
        assert len(runtime.queue) == (1 if stamp == 700 else 0)
    runtime.pause()
    assert len(runtime.queue) == 0
    assert runtime.process_one() is None


def test_default_runtime_cannot_issue_actions():
    runtime = ActionRuntime()
    assert runtime.safety.paused
    assert not runtime.safety.config["safety"]["enabled"]
    assert not runtime.executor.handlers
    assert runtime.executor.dry_run


def test_tracking_can_expire_during_context_read():
    safety, clock, detector = ready()
    request = safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect())
    original_detect = detector.detect
    calls = []

    def slow_detect(mode):
        snapshot = original_detect(mode)
        clock.value += 200
        return snapshot

    detector.detect = slow_detect
    outcome = ActionExecutor(safety, detector, {"teams.toggle_mute": lambda: calls.append(1)})
    assert outcome.execute(request).reason == "authorization_changed"
    assert not calls

========== PLIK: tests\test_analysis_pipeline.py ==========
from dataclasses import replace

import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.core.analysis_pipeline import AnalysisPipeline
from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.definitions import Gesture, Motion, Quality
from tests.gesture_samples import frame, low_score, sample


def test_fist_event_and_no_repeat():
    p = AnalysisPipeline()
    events = [
        p.process(frame(sample(Gesture.FIST), i * 50), 100, 100).gesture_event for i in range(10)
    ]
    assert events.count(Gesture.FIST) == 1


def test_loss_low_quality_multiple():
    p = AnalysisPipeline()
    assert p.process(frame(None, 0), 100, 100).gesture == Gesture.NONE
    assert p.process(frame(low_score(sample()), 10), 100, 100).quality == Quality.LOW_CONFIDENCE
    two = replace(frame(sample(), 20), hands=(sample(), sample(side="Left")))
    result = p.process(two, 100, 100)
    assert result.quality == Quality.MULTIPLE_HANDS
    assert result.gesture_event is None
    assert result.motion == Motion.NONE


def test_swipe_pipeline_and_no_static_event_during_motion_cooldown():
    p = AnalysisPipeline()
    results = [
        p.process(frame(sample(scale=0.5, dx=-0.2 + i * 0.1), i * 100), 100, 100) for i in range(4)
    ]
    assert results[-1].motion_event == Motion.SWIPE_RIGHT
    assert results[-1].gesture_event is None
    assert results[-1].cooldown_ms == 1500


def test_gap_side_switch_and_timestamp():
    p = AnalysisPipeline()
    p.process(frame(sample(), 0), 100, 100)
    result = p.process(frame(sample(side="Left"), 1000), 100, 100)
    assert result.motion == Motion.NONE
    assert result.gesture_event is None
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        p.process(frame(sample(), 1000), 100, 100)

from src.gesture_assistant.app.app_state import AppState
from src.gesture_assistant.domain.enums import SessionState


def test_initial_state_is_idle() -> None:
    state = AppState()

    assert state.state == SessionState.IDLE
    assert state.status_message == "Gotowy"
    assert state.error_message == ""


def test_state_change_emits_signal() -> None:
    state = AppState()
    changes = []
    state.state_changed.connect(changes.append)

    state.set_state(SessionState.RUNNING)
    state.set_state(SessionState.RUNNING)

    assert changes == [SessionState.RUNNING]
    assert state.state == SessionState.RUNNING


def test_status_and_error_signals() -> None:
    state = AppState()
    statuses = []
    errors = []

    state.status_message_changed.connect(statuses.append)
    state.error_occurred.connect(errors.append)

    state.set_status_message("Śledzenie")
    state.set_error_message("Błąd kamery")

    assert statuses == ["Śledzenie"]
    assert errors == ["Błąd kamery"]
    assert state.status_message == "Śledzenie"
    assert state.error_message == "Błąd kamery"

========== PLIK: tests\test_camera_worker.py ==========
import threading

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.vision.camera_worker import CameraWorker
from src.gesture_assistant.vision.hand_provider import HandResult


class FakeHandProvider:
    def __init__(self):
        self.seen = []
        self.processed = threading.Semaphore(0)
        self.closed = False

    def process(self, frame_bgr, timestamp_ms):
        self.seen.append((int(frame_bgr[0, 0, 0]), timestamp_ms))
        self.processed.release()
        return HandResult(len(self.seen), timestamp_ms, ())

    def close(self):
        self.closed = True


class FakeCapture:
    def __init__(self, frames, opened=True, provider=None):
        self.frames = list(frames)
        self.opened = opened
        self.provider = provider
        self.index = 0
        self.released = False

    def isOpened(self):
        return self.opened

    def read(self):
        if self.provider is not None and self.index:
            assert self.provider.processed.acquire(timeout=3), "Brak potwierdzenia analizy."
        if self.index == len(self.frames):
            return False, None
        frame = self.frames[self.index]
        self.index += 1
        return True, frame

    def release(self):
        self.released = True


def make_frame(value):
    return np.full((24, 32, 3), value, dtype=np.uint8)


def finish(worker, qapp):
    assert worker.wait(5000), "Worker nie zakończył pracy."
    qapp.processEvents()
    assert not worker.isRunning()


def test_worker_processes_frames_and_releases_capture(qapp):
    provider = FakeHandProvider()
    capture = FakeCapture([make_frame(i) for i in range(3)], provider=provider)
    worker = CameraWorker(0, provider, capture_factory=lambda _: capture)
    frames, results, errors = [], [], []
    worker.frame_ready.connect(lambda f, t: frames.append((f, t)))
    worker.result_ready.connect(results.append)
    worker.error.connect(errors.append)
    try:
        worker.start()
        finish(worker, qapp)
        assert len(frames) == 3
        assert len(results) == 3
        assert errors
        assert capture.released and provider.closed
        timestamps = [t for _, t in frames]
        assert timestamps == sorted(set(timestamps))
        assert [int(f[0, 0, 0]) for f, _ in frames] == [0, 1, 2]
    finally:
        worker.stop()
        assert worker.wait(5000)


def test_worker_reports_camera_open_error(qapp):
    provider = FakeHandProvider()
    capture = FakeCapture([], opened=False)
    worker = CameraWorker(0, provider, capture_factory=lambda _: capture)
    errors = []
    worker.error.connect(errors.append)
    worker.start()
    finish(worker, qapp)
    assert len(errors) == 1
    assert "Nie można otworzyć kamery" in errors[0]
    assert capture.released and provider.closed


@pytest.mark.parametrize("source", ["bad.mp4", "0", -1, True])
def test_worker_rejects_non_camera_sources(source):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="numer kamery"):
        CameraWorker(source, FakeHandProvider())


def test_worker_drops_old_frames_when_analysis_is_slow(qapp):
    entered, release, produced = threading.Event(), threading.Event(), threading.Event()

    class BlockedProvider(FakeHandProvider):
        def process(self, frame_bgr, timestamp_ms):
            if not self.seen:
                entered.set()
                assert release.wait(3), "Nie zwolniono kontrolowanej analizy."
            return super().process(frame_bgr, timestamp_ms)

    class BurstCapture(FakeCapture):
        def read(self):
            if self.index == len(self.frames):
                # Entering the next read proves the last frame was published.
                produced.set()
                return False, None
            if self.index == 1:
                assert entered.wait(3), "Analiza pierwszej klatki nie wystartowała."
            return super().read()

    provider = BlockedProvider()
    capture = BurstCapture([make_frame(i) for i in range(20)])
    worker = CameraWorker(0, provider, capture_factory=lambda _: capture)
    try:
        worker.start()
        assert produced.wait(3)
        release.set()
        finish(worker, qapp)
        assert [value for value, _ in provider.seen] == [0, 19]
        assert 1 <= len(provider.seen) < 20
        assert capture.released and provider.closed
    finally:
        release.set()
        worker.stop()
        assert worker.wait(5000)


def test_worker_stop_releases_capture(qapp):
    entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()

    class WaitingCapture(FakeCapture):
        def read(self):
            entered.set()
            assert release.wait(3)
            return True, make_frame(0)

    provider = FakeHandProvider()
    capture = WaitingCapture([])
    worker = CameraWorker(0, provider, capture_factory=lambda _: capture)
    try:
        worker.start()
        assert entered.wait(3)
        worker.stop()
        release.set()
        finish(worker, qapp)
        assert capture.released and provider.closed
        assert not worker.isRunning()
    finally:
        release.set()
        worker.stop()
        assert worker.wait(5000)


def test_provider_error_still_closes_resources(qapp):
    class FailingProvider(FakeHandProvider):
        def process(self, frame_bgr, timestamp_ms):
            raise RuntimeError("Kontrolowany błąd testowy")

    provider = FailingProvider()
    capture = FakeCapture([make_frame(1)])
    worker = CameraWorker(0, provider, capture_factory=lambda _: capture)
    errors = []
    worker.error.connect(errors.append)
    worker.start()
    finish(worker, qapp)
    assert any("Kontrolowany błąd testowy" in message for message in errors)
    assert capture.released and provider.closed

========== PLIK: tests\test_config_manager.py ==========
import json
from unittest.mock import patch

import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.config.schema import ConfigVersionError, validate_config
from src.gesture_assistant.core.config_manager import ConfigManager, defaults


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "section, key, bad",
    [
        ("camera", "index", True),
        ("camera", "fps_limit", 0),
        ("camera", "width", "1280"),
        ("thresholds", "gesture_hold_ms", 699),
        ("thresholds", "action_cooldown_ms", 1999),
        ("thresholds", "motion_cooldown_ms", 1499),
        ("thresholds", "min_confidence", float("nan")),
        ("safety", "paused", 1),
    ],
)
def test_strict_validation(section, key, bad):
    config = defaults()
    config[section][key] = bad
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_config(config)


def test_defaults_and_roundtrip(tmp_path):
    manager = ConfigManager(tmp_path / "config.json")
    config = manager.load().config
    assert config["safety"]["paused"] and not config["safety"]["enabled"]
    assert not config["safety"]["sleep_computer"]
    config["mode"] = "Browser"
    manager.save(config)
    assert manager.load().config == config
    assert not list(tmp_path.glob(".config_*"))


def test_corrupt_backup_and_repair(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "config.json"
    old = b"{broken JSON"
    path.write_bytes(old)
    result = ConfigManager(path).load()
    assert result.restored and "domyślne" in result.message
    assert result.backup.read_bytes() == old
    assert json.loads(path.read_text())["schema_version"] == 1


def test_future_version_preserved(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "config.json"
    config = defaults()
    config["schema_version"] = 99
    old = json.dumps(config)
    path.write_text(old)
    result = ConfigManager(path).load()
    assert result.restored
    assert path.read_text() == old
    assert result.backup.read_text() == old
    with pytest.raises(ConfigVersionError):
        validate_config(config)


def test_failed_replace_preserves_previous_settings(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "config.json"
    manager = ConfigManager(path)
    manager.save(defaults())
    old = path.read_bytes()
    config = defaults()
    config["mode"] = "Media"
    with patch("src.gesture_assistant.core.config_manager.os.replace", side_effect=OSError("disk")):
        with pytest.raises(OSError):
            manager.save(config)
    assert path.read_bytes() == old
    assert not list(tmp_path.glob(".config_*"))


def test_backup_failure_does_not_overwrite(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "config.json"
    path.write_bytes(b"broken")
    target = "src.gesture_assistant.core.config_manager.shutil.copy2"
    with patch(target, side_effect=OSError("disk")):
        with pytest.raises(OSError):
            ConfigManager(path).load()
    assert path.read_bytes() == b"broken"


def test_reset_preserves_old_and_duplicate_json_is_rejected(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "config.json"
    path.write_text('{"schema_version":1,"schema_version":2}')
    result = ConfigManager(path).load()
    assert result.restored and result.backup.exists()
    reset = ConfigManager(path).reset()
    assert reset.backup.exists()
    assert reset.config == defaults()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["Auto", "Teams", "Browser", "Presentation", "Media", "System"])
def test_all_modes(mode):
    config = defaults()
    config["mode"] = mode
    assert validate_config(config)["mode"] == mode


def test_invalid_shortcut_and_application_in_config(tmp_path):
    config = defaults()
    config["targets"]["shortcuts"] = {"mute": "ctrl++m"}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_config(config)
    config = defaults()
    config["targets"]["applications"] = {"app": str(tmp_path / "missing.exe")}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_config(config)

import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.core.context_detector import ContextDetector, Foreground
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.contracts import ContextKind
from tests.action_samples import Clock, context


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "executable, expected",
    [
        ("ms-teams.exe", ContextKind.TEAMS),
        ("Teams.exe", ContextKind.TEAMS),
        ("chrome.exe", ContextKind.BROWSER),
        ("powerpnt.exe", ContextKind.PRESENTATION),
        ("spotify.exe", ContextKind.MEDIA),
        ("explorer.exe", ContextKind.SYSTEM),
        ("fake-teams.exe", ContextKind.SYSTEM),
    ],
)
def test_detect_exact_process(executable, expected):
    assert context(Clock(), executable).detect().kind == expected


def test_unknown_and_manual_mode_does_not_override_teams():
    c = Clock()
    assert ContextDetector(lambda: None, c).detect().kind == ContextKind.UNKNOWN
    snapshot = context(c).detect("System")
    assert snapshot.kind == ContextKind.TEAMS
    assert snapshot.system_blocked
    assert context(c, "chrome.exe").detect().system_blocked


def test_reader_failure_and_invalid_identity():
    def fail():
        raise OSError("permission denied")

    assert ContextDetector(fail).detect().kind == ContextKind.UNKNOWN
    invalid = ContextDetector(lambda: Foreground(0, 1, "ms-teams.exe")).detect()
    assert invalid.kind == ContextKind.UNKNOWN

    [

        ("wmplayer.exe", ContextKind.MEDIA),
        ("msedgewebview2.exe", ContextKind.BROWSER),

)
def test_presentation_media_and_webview(executable, expected):

def test_malformed_executable_is_not_system_fallback(path):

========== PLIK: tests\test_context_reader.py ==========
import ctypes
import sys
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.core import context_detector


class FakeFunction:
    def __init__(self, function):
        self.function = function

    def __call__(self, *args):
        return self.function(*args)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "query_ok, changed, own",
    [
        (True, False, False),
        (False, False, False),
        (True, True, False),
        (True, False, True),
    ],
)
def test_pygetwindow_reader_closes_process_handle(monkeypatch, query_ok, changed, own):
    from ctypes import wintypes

    closed = []
    foreground_calls = []

    def foreground():
        foreground_calls.append(1)
        return 12 if changed and len(foreground_calls) > 1 else 11

    def pid(hwnd, pointer):
        ctypes.cast(pointer, ctypes.POINTER(wintypes.DWORD))[0] = 42
        return 1

    def query(handle, flags, buffer, size):
        buffer.value = "C:/Apps/POWERPNT.EXE"
        return query_ok

    user = SimpleNamespace(
        GetForegroundWindow=FakeFunction(foreground),
        GetWindowThreadProcessId=FakeFunction(pid),
    )
    kernel = SimpleNamespace(
        OpenProcess=FakeFunction(lambda *args: 7),
        QueryFullProcessImageNameW=FakeFunction(query),
        CloseHandle=FakeFunction(lambda handle: closed.append(handle) or 1),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        context_detector, "os", SimpleNamespace(name="nt", getpid=lambda: 42 if own else 999)
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        ctypes, "WinDLL", lambda name, **kw: user if name == "user32" else kernel, raising=False
    )
    monkeypatch.setitem(
        sys.modules,
        "pygetwindow",
        SimpleNamespace(
            getActiveWindow=lambda: SimpleNamespace(_hWnd=11),
        ),
    )
    result = context_detector.read_windows_foreground()
    assert closed == [7]
    if query_ok and not changed:
        assert result.hwnd == 11 and result.pid == 42
        assert result.executable == "C:/Apps/POWERPNT.EXE"
    else:
        assert result is None

========== PLIK: tests\test_debounce.py ==========
import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.core.debounce import CooldownManager, Debouncer
from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.definitions import Gesture


def test_five_frames_then_latched():
    d = Debouncer()
    for i in range(4):
        assert d.update(Gesture.FIST, i * 20) is None
    assert d.update(Gesture.FIST, 80) == Gesture.FIST
    assert d.update(Gesture.FIST, 5000) is None


def test_time_alternative_and_release():
    d = Debouncer()
    assert d.update(Gesture.FIST, 0) is None
    assert d.update(Gesture.FIST, 700) == Gesture.FIST
    assert d.update(Gesture.NONE, 800) is None
    assert d.update(Gesture.NONE, 1800) is None
    assert d.update(Gesture.FIST, 1801) is None
    assert d.update(Gesture.FIST, 2501) == Gesture.FIST


def test_change_jitter_and_short_release():
    d = Debouncer()
    for i in range(30):
        g = Gesture.FIST if i % 2 else Gesture.PINCH
        assert d.update(g, i * 20) is None
    assert d.update(Gesture.NONE, 900) is None
    for i in range(5):
        event = d.update(Gesture.FIST, 1000 + i)
    assert event == Gesture.FIST
    d.update(Gesture.NONE, 1100)
    assert d.update(Gesture.FIST, 2000) is None
    assert d.update(Gesture.FIST, 5000) is None


def test_cooldown_and_timestamp():
    c = CooldownManager()
    c.start("x", 100, 1500)
    assert c.remaining("x", 1599) == 1
    assert c.remaining("x", 1600) == 0
    d = Debouncer()
    d.update(Gesture.NONE, 1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        d.update(Gesture.NONE, 1)


def test_tracking_discontinuity_does_not_prove_release():
    d = Debouncer()
    for stamp in range(5):
        event = d.update(Gesture.FIST, stamp)
    assert event == Gesture.FIST
    d.update(Gesture.NONE, 100)
    d.tracking_discontinuity()
    d.update(Gesture.NONE, 5000)
    assert d.update(Gesture.FIST, 5100) is None
    assert d.update(Gesture.FIST, 5800) is None

========== PLIK: tests\test_gesture_classifier.py ==========
from dataclasses import replace

import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.classifier import GestureClassifier
from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.definitions import Gesture, Quality
from src.gesture_assistant.vision.hand_provider import HandPoint
from tests.gesture_samples import low_score, sample


@pytest.mark.parametrize("gesture", [g for g in Gesture if g not in (Gesture.NONE,)])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("side", ["Left", "Right"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("scale", [0.7, 1.0])
def test_static_gestures_and_unknown(gesture, side, scale):
    result = GestureClassifier().classify(sample(gesture, side, scale=scale))
    assert result.gesture == gesture
    assert result.quality == Quality.VALID


def test_none_is_not_unknown():
    assert GestureClassifier().classify(None).gesture == Gesture.NONE
    assert GestureClassifier().classify(sample(Gesture.UNKNOWN)).gesture == Gesture.UNKNOWN


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", [float("nan"), float("inf")])
def test_nonfinite(value):
    hand = sample()
    points = list(hand.landmarks)
    points[8] = HandPoint(value, 0.2, 0)
    invalid = replace(hand, landmarks=tuple(points))
    assert GestureClassifier().classify(invalid).gesture == Gesture.NONE


def test_zero_vectors_and_low_score():
    hand = sample()
    zero = replace(hand, landmarks=(HandPoint(0.5, 0.5, 0),) * 21)
    assert GestureClassifier().classify(zero).quality == Quality.INVALID
    assert GestureClassifier().classify(low_score(hand)).quality == Quality.LOW_CONFIDENCE


def test_aspect_ratio():
    hand = sample(Gesture.VICTORY)
    # Same physical geometry in a 2:1 frame: normalized x is halved.
    hand = replace(hand, landmarks=tuple(HandPoint(p.x / 2, p.y, p.z) for p in hand.landmarks))
    assert GestureClassifier().classify(hand, 200, 100).gesture == Gesture.VICTORY


def test_missing_landmarks_and_out_of_frame():
    hand = sample()
    assert GestureClassifier().classify(replace(hand, landmarks=())).gesture == Gesture.NONE
    assert GestureClassifier().classify(sample(dx=0.7)).quality == Quality.OUT_OF_FRAME


def test_thumb_direction_requires_vertical_view():
    hand = sample(Gesture.ROCK)
    # This horizontal thumb + little finger must not be treated as thumbs-up.
    assert GestureClassifier().classify(hand).gesture == Gesture.ROCK

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.vision.hand_provider import (
    Hand,
    HandPoint,
    HandResult,
    MediaPipeHandProvider,
    validate_input,
)


def test_missing_hand_model(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="modelu dłoni"):
        MediaPipeHandProvider(tmp_path / "missing.task")


def test_hand_contract_does_not_require_body():
    points = tuple(HandPoint(0.5, 0.5, 0.0) for _ in range(21))
    hand = Hand(points, points, "Left", 0.9)
    result = HandResult(1, 123, (hand,))
    assert len(result.hands[0].landmarks) == 21
    assert result.timestamp_ms == 123


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "frame",
    [
        np.zeros((0, 2, 3), dtype=np.uint8),
        np.zeros((2, 2), dtype=np.uint8),
        np.zeros((2, 2, 4), dtype=np.uint8),
        np.zeros((2, 2, 3), dtype=np.float32),
    ],
)
def test_reject_invalid_images(frame):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_input(frame, 1)


def test_valid_input():
    validate_input(np.zeros((24, 32, 3), dtype=np.uint8), 100)


def test_invalid_timestamp():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_input(np.zeros((24, 32, 3), dtype=np.uint8), -1)

========== PLIK: tests\test_hand_provider_runtime.py ==========
import sys
from types import ModuleType, SimpleNamespace

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.vision.hand_provider import MediaPipeHandProvider


def provider_with_sdk(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    cv2 = ModuleType("cv2")
    cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB = 1
    cv2.cvtColor = lambda frame, code: frame[:, :, ::-1].copy()
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "cv2", cv2)
    detector = SimpleNamespace(
        timestamps=[],
        closed=False,
    )

    def detect(image, timestamp):
        detector.timestamps.append(timestamp)
        return SimpleNamespace(hand_landmarks=[], handedness=[], hand_world_landmarks=[])

    def close():
        detector.closed = True

    detector.detect_for_video = detect
    detector.close = close
    mp = ModuleType("mediapipe")
    tasks = ModuleType("mediapipe.tasks")
    python = ModuleType("mediapipe.tasks.python")
    vision = ModuleType("mediapipe.tasks.python.vision")
    mp.Image = lambda **kwargs: kwargs
    mp.ImageFormat = SimpleNamespace(SRGB=1)
    python.BaseOptions = lambda **kwargs: kwargs
    vision.HandLandmarkerOptions = lambda **kwargs: kwargs
    vision.RunningMode = SimpleNamespace(VIDEO=1)
    vision.HandLandmarker = SimpleNamespace(create_from_options=lambda _: detector)
    mp.tasks, tasks.python, python.vision = tasks, python, vision
    for name, module in (
        ("mediapipe", mp),
        ("mediapipe.tasks", tasks),
        ("mediapipe.tasks.python", python),
        ("mediapipe.tasks.python.vision", vision),
    ):
        monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, name, module)
    model = tmp_path / "fake.task"
    model.write_bytes(b"test-double-not-a-real-model")
    return MediaPipeHandProvider(model), detector


def test_strict_timestamps_and_close(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    provider, detector = provider_with_sdk(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    image = np.zeros((24, 32, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
    assert provider.process(image, 100).hands == ()
    for timestamp in (100, 99):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="rosnące"):
            provider.process(image, timestamp)
    assert provider.process(image, 101).timestamp_ms == 101
    assert detector.timestamps == [100, 101]
    provider.close()
    provider.close()
    assert detector.closed
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        provider.process(image, 102)

========== PLIK: tests\test_integrations_final.py ==========
import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.config.schema import validate_config
from src.gesture_assistant.core.action_executor import ActionExecutor
from src.gesture_assistant.core.config_manager import defaults
from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.definitions import Gesture
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations import browser_actions
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.dispatcher import NativeDispatcher
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.keyboard_actions import validate_shortcut
from tests.action_samples import feed, policy, ready
from tests.integration_samples import FakeKeyboard

ACTIONS = [
    ("presentation.next_slide", "powerpnt.exe", "right"),
    ("presentation.previous_slide", "powerpnt.exe", "left"),
    ("presentation.start_presentation", "powerpnt.exe", "f5"),
    ("presentation.end_presentation", "powerpnt.exe", "escape"),
    ("media.play_pause", "vlc.exe", "media_play_pause"),
    ("media.stop", "vlc.exe", "media_stop"),
    ("media.volume_up", "spotify.exe", "volume_up"),
    ("media.volume_down", "wmplayer.exe", "volume_down"),
    ("media.next_track", "vlc.exe", "media_next_track"),
    ("media.previous_track", "vlc.exe", "media_previous_track"),
]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("action_id, executable, expected", ACTIONS)
def test_authorized_executor_to_dispatcher_with_fake_keyboard(action_id, executable, expected):
    safety, clock, detector = ready(action_id, executable)
    keyboard = FakeKeyboard()
    dispatcher = NativeDispatcher(safety.config, keyboard)
    request = safety.request(action_id, "FIST", detector.detect())
    assert request is not None
    executor = ActionExecutor(safety, detector, dry_run=False, dispatcher=dispatcher)
    outcome = executor.execute(request)
    assert outcome.executed and keyboard.calls[0][0] == expected
    assert not executor.execute(request).executed


@pytest.mark.parametrize("action_id, executable, expected", ACTIONS)
def test_paused_and_teams_context_block_native_path(action_id, executable, expected):
    safety, clock, detector = ready(action_id, executable)
    request = safety.request(action_id, "FIST", detector.detect())
    keyboard = FakeKeyboard()
    executor = ActionExecutor(
        safety,
        detector,
        dry_run=False,
        dispatcher=NativeDispatcher(safety.config, keyboard),
    )
    safety.pause()
    assert not executor.execute(request).executed
    assert not keyboard.calls
    safety, clock, detector = ready(action_id, "ms-teams.exe")
    assert safety.request(action_id, "FIST", detector.detect()) is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("action_id, executable, expected", ACTIONS)
def test_action_cooldown_applies_across_different_gestures(action_id, executable, expected):
    safety, clock, detector = ready(action_id, executable)
    request = safety.request(action_id, "FIST", detector.detect())
    assert safety.reserve(request, detector.detect()) == ""
    safety.finish(request)
    feed(safety, clock, 800, 1500, Gesture.OPEN_PALM)
    assert safety.request(action_id, "OPEN_PALM", detector.detect()) is None
    feed(safety, clock, 1600, 2700, Gesture.OPEN_PALM)
    assert safety.request(action_id, "OPEN_PALM", detector.detect()) is not None


def test_forced_mode_does_not_send_media_keys_to_powerpoint():
    config = policy("media.play_pause")
    config["mode"] = "Media"
    safety, clock, detector = ready("media.play_pause", "powerpnt.exe", config)
    assert detector.detect("Media").profile.value == "Media"
    assert safety.request("media.play_pause", "FIST", detector.detect("Media")) is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "value",
    [
        "",
        "javascript:alert(1)",
        "file:///tmp/file",
        "https://x:bad/",
        "https://user:secret@example.com",
        "https://a b",
    ],
)
def test_invalid_url_never_opens_browser(monkeypatch, value):
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_actions.webbrowser, "open", lambda *a, **k: calls.append(a))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        browser_actions.open_url(value, lambda: True)
    assert not calls


def test_valid_url_uses_guard_and_reports_handler_failure(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_actions.webbrowser, "open", lambda *a, **k: False)
    with pytest.raises(OSError):
        browser_actions.open_url("https://example.com", lambda: True)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("shortcut", ["ctrl++m", "ctrl+ctrl+m", "ctrl+alt+delete", "unknown", ""])
def test_invalid_shortcut(shortcut):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_shortcut(shortcut)


def test_configuration_contains_new_profiles_but_remains_disabled():
    config = defaults()
    assert config["profiles"]["Presentation"]["SWIPE_RIGHT"] == "presentation.next_slide"
    assert config["profiles"]["Media"]["FIST"] == "media.play_pause"
    assert config["safety"]["paused"] and not config["safety"]["enabled"]
    assert config["execution"]["dry_run"] and not config["safety"]["sleep_computer"]
    assert validate_config(config) == config


@pytest.mark.parametrize("action_id, executable, expected", ACTIONS)
def test_dry_run_never_calls_native_backend(action_id, executable, expected):
    safety, clock, detector = ready(action_id, executable)
    keyboard = FakeKeyboard()
    request = safety.request(action_id, "FIST", detector.detect())
    executor = ActionExecutor(
        safety,
        detector,
        dry_run=True,
        dispatcher=NativeDispatcher(safety.config, keyboard),
    )
    result = executor.execute(request)
    assert result.accepted and not result.executed
    assert result.reason == "dry_run_no_side_effect"
    assert not keyboard.calls

import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.config.schema import validate_config
from src.gesture_assistant.core.config_manager import defaults
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations import media_actions as media
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.keyboard_actions import (
    MEDIA_VIRTUAL_KEYS,
    validate_action_shortcut,
)
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.native_backend import AuthorizationLost, WindowsBackend
from tests.integration_samples import FakeKeyboard

CASES = [
    ("play_pause", "media_play_pause", 0xB3),
    ("stop", "media_stop", 0xB2),
    ("volume_up", "volume_up", 0xAF),
    ("volume_down", "volume_down", 0xAE),
    ("next_track", "media_next_track", 0xB0),
    ("previous_track", "media_previous_track", 0xB1),
]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name, key, vk", CASES)
def test_media_default_and_vk(name, key, vk):
    keyboard = FakeKeyboard()
    getattr(media, name)(keyboard, lambda: True)
    assert keyboard.calls == [(key, (key,), False)]
    assert MEDIA_VIRTUAL_KEYS[key] == vk


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name, key, vk", CASES)
def test_media_overrides(name, key, vk):
    keyboard = FakeKeyboard()
    action_id = f"media.{name}"
    getattr(media, name)(keyboard, lambda: True, {action_id: "ctrl+shift+p"})
    assert keyboard.calls[0][0] == "ctrl+shift+p"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name, key, vk", CASES)
def test_media_guard_blocks(name, key, vk):
    keyboard = FakeKeyboard()
    with pytest.raises(AuthorizationLost):
        getattr(media, name)(keyboard, lambda: False)
    assert not keyboard.calls


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name, key, vk", CASES)
def test_backend_mapping_without_native_execution(monkeypatch, name, key, vk):
    from src.gesture_assistant.integrations import native_backend

    calls = []

    def fake_send(keys, guard):
        assert guard()
        calls.append(keys)

    monkeypatch.setattr(native_backend, "send_virtual_keys", fake_send)
    WindowsBackend().hotkey(key, lambda: True)
    assert calls == [[vk]]


def test_media_keys_cannot_hide_inside_teams_profile():
    config = defaults()
    config["targets"]["shortcuts"]["teams.toggle_mute"] = "volume_up"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="tylko dla akcji Media"):
        validate_config(config)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="modyfikatorów"):
        validate_action_shortcut("media.volume_up", "ctrl+volume_up")

========== PLIK: tests\test_motion_classifier.py ==========
import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.definitions import Gesture, Motion
from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.motion_classifier import MotionClassifier


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "delta, expected",
    [
        ((0.1, 0), Motion.SWIPE_RIGHT),
        ((-0.1, 0), Motion.SWIPE_LEFT),
        ((0, 0.1), Motion.SWIPE_DOWN),
        ((0, -0.1), Motion.SWIPE_UP),
    ],
)
def test_four_swipes(delta, expected):
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    result = Motion.NONE
    for i in range(4):
        result = classifier.update(
            (0.5 + i * delta[0], 0.5 + i * delta[1]), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, i * 100
        )
    assert result == expected
    assert classifier.update((0.8, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 400) == Motion.NONE


@pytest.mark.parametrize("gesture", [Gesture.FIST, Gesture.UNKNOWN, Gesture.NONE])
def test_only_open_palm(gesture):
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    for i in range(4):
        assert classifier.update((0.2 + i * 0.1, 0.5), gesture, i * 100) == Motion.NONE


def test_too_slow_diagonal_jitter_and_teleport():
    for points, times in [
        ([(0.2 + i * 0.1, 0.5) for i in range(4)], [0, 300, 600, 900]),
        ([(0.2 + i * 0.1, 0.2 + i * 0.1) for i in range(4)], [0, 100, 200, 300]),
        ([(0.5, 0.5), (0.51, 0.5), (0.49, 0.5), (0.51, 0.5)], [0, 100, 200, 300]),
        ([(0.1, 0.5), (0.7, 0.5), (0.71, 0.5)], [0, 100, 200]),
    ]:
        classifier = MotionClassifier()
        for point, timestamp in zip(points, times, strict=True):
            assert classifier.update(point, Gesture.OPEN_PALM, timestamp) == Motion.NONE


def test_gesture_change_resets_trajectory():
    c = MotionClassifier()
    assert c.update((0.2, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 0) == Motion.NONE
    assert c.update((0.3, 0.5), Gesture.FIST, 100) == Motion.NONE
    assert c.update((0.5, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 200) == Motion.NONE


def test_threshold_not_crossed():
    c = MotionClassifier()
    for i, x in enumerate((0.2, 0.3, 0.449, 0.448)):
        assert c.update((x, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, i * 100) == Motion.NONE


def test_cooldown_expiry_and_missing_points():
    c = MotionClassifier()
    for i in range(4):
        result = c.update((0.2 + i * 0.1, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, i * 100)
    assert result == Motion.SWIPE_RIGHT
    assert c.update(None, Gesture.NONE, 400) == Motion.NONE
    for i in range(4):
        result = c.update((0.2 + i * 0.1, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 1800 + i * 100)
    assert result == Motion.SWIPE_RIGHT


def test_backtracking_not_smooth():
    c = MotionClassifier()
    # Displacement 0.3, path 0.5: efficiency below 0.85.
    for i, x in enumerate((0.1, 0.25, 0.15, 0.3, 0.4)):
        assert c.update((x, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, i * 100) == Motion.NONE


def test_trajectory_longer_than_window_never_reaches_distance():
    c = MotionClassifier()
    for i in range(5):
        assert c.update((0.2 + i * 0.0625, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, i * 200) == Motion.NONE

========== KONIEC PLIKU ==========

========== PLIK: tests\test_open_url_execution.py ==========
import os
import threading
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.core.action_runtime import ActionRuntime
from src.gesture_assistant.core.analysis_pipeline import AnalysisPipeline
from src.gesture_assistant.core.context_detector import ContextDetector, Foreground
from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.definitions import Gesture, Quality
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations import ACTION_CATALOG, browser_actions
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.contracts import ContextKind
from tests.action_samples import Clock, analysis, policy
from tests.gesture_samples import frame, sample


def url_runtime(mode="Browser", pid=None, executable="python.exe", dry_run=False):
    clock = Clock()
    config = policy("browser.open_url")
    config["mode"] = mode
    config["execution"]["dry_run"] = dry_run
    config["bindings"] = {"OPEN_PALM": "browser.open_url"}
    config["targets"]["urls"]["browser.open_url"] = "https://example.com/watch"
    foreground = Foreground(71, os.getpid() if pid is None else pid, "C:/Apps/" + executable)
    detector = ContextDetector(lambda: foreground, clock)
    return ActionRuntime(config, detector, clock), clock


def hold(runtime, clock, start=0, end=700, gesture=Gesture.OPEN_PALM):
    for stamp in range(start, end + 1, 100):
        clock.value = stamp
        runtime.observe(analysis(stamp, gesture))


def test_open_palm_from_assistant_calls_webbrowser_once(monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        browser_actions.webbrowser,
        "open",
        lambda url, **kwargs: calls.append((url, kwargs)) or True,
    )
    runtime, clock = url_runtime()
    hold(runtime, clock)
    outcome = runtime.process_one()
    assert outcome is not None and outcome.executed
    assert calls == [("https://example.com/watch", {"new": 2, "autoraise": False})]
    hold(runtime, clock, 800, 2000)
    assert runtime.process_one() is None
    assert len(calls) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["Auto", "Teams", "Browser", "Presentation", "Media", "System"])
def test_explicit_url_binding_launches_without_requiring_browser_focus(monkeypatch, mode):
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_actions.webbrowser, "open", lambda *a, **k: calls.append(a) or True)
    runtime, clock = url_runtime(mode, 42, "explorer.exe")
    hold(runtime, clock)
    outcome = runtime.process_one()
    assert outcome.executed and len(calls) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "action",
    [
        "browser.new_tab",
        "system.lock",
        "teams.toggle_mute",
        "media.play_pause",
        "presentation.next_slide",
    ],
)
def test_own_window_never_accepts_targeted_shortcuts(action):
    runtime, clock = url_runtime()
    config = runtime.safety.config
    config["mode"] = ACTION_CATALOG[action].context.value
    config["allowed_actions"] = [action]
    config["bindings"] = {"OPEN_PALM": action}
    runtime.apply_config(config)
    runtime.resume()
    hold(runtime, clock)
    assert runtime.detector.detect(config["mode"]).kind == ContextKind.UNKNOWN
    assert len(runtime.queue) == 0
    assert runtime.diagnostics()["reason"] == "assistant_window_not_target"


def test_url_unknown_foreground_fails_closed(monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_actions.webbrowser, "open", lambda *a, **k: calls.append(a))
    runtime, clock = url_runtime()
    runtime.detector.reader = lambda: None
    hold(runtime, clock)
    assert runtime.process_one() is None
    assert runtime.diagnostics()["reason"] == "unknown_or_stale_context"
    assert not calls


@pytest.mark.parametrize("condition", ["paused", "disabled", "not_allowed", "dry_run"])
def test_url_still_requires_explicit_authorization(monkeypatch, condition):
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_actions.webbrowser, "open", lambda *a, **k: calls.append(a) or True)
    runtime, clock = url_runtime(dry_run=condition == "dry_run")
    if condition == "paused":
        runtime.pause()
    elif condition in ("disabled", "not_allowed"):
        config = runtime.safety.config
        if condition == "disabled":
            config["safety"]["enabled"] = False
        else:
            config["allowed_actions"] = []
        runtime.apply_config(config)
        runtime.resume()
    hold(runtime, clock)
    outcome = runtime.process_one()
    if condition == "dry_run":
        assert outcome.accepted and not outcome.executed
    else:
        assert outcome is None
    assert not calls


def test_url_waits_full_700ms(monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_actions.webbrowser, "open", lambda *a, **k: calls.append(a) or True)
    runtime, clock = url_runtime()
    hold(runtime, clock, end=600)
    assert runtime.process_one() is None
    clock.value = 699
    runtime.observe(analysis(699, Gesture.OPEN_PALM))
    assert runtime.process_one() is None
    clock.value = 700
    runtime.observe(analysis(700, Gesture.OPEN_PALM))
    assert runtime.process_one().executed and len(calls) == 1


def test_real_consumer_thread_reaches_fake_browser(monkeypatch):
    called = threading.Event()
    calls = []

    def fake_open(url, **kwargs):
        calls.append(url)
        called.set()
        return True

    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_actions.webbrowser, "open", fake_open)
    runtime, clock = url_runtime()
    runtime.start()
    try:
        hold(runtime, clock)
        assert called.wait(2), "Konsument nie dotarł do browser.open_url."
    finally:
        runtime.stop()
        runtime._thread.join(timeout=2)
    assert not runtime.is_running
    assert runtime.status().executed and len(calls) == 1


def test_real_pipeline_to_url_from_own_window(monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_actions.webbrowser, "open", lambda *a, **k: calls.append(a) or True)
    runtime, clock = url_runtime()
    pipeline = AnalysisPipeline()
    for stamp in range(0, 701, 100):
        clock.value = stamp
        runtime.observe(pipeline.process(frame(sample(Gesture.OPEN_PALM), stamp), 100, 100))
    assert runtime.process_one().executed
    assert len(calls) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "smoothed, raw, expected, confidence",
    [
        (Gesture.UNKNOWN, Gesture.OPEN_PALM, Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 0.85),
        (Gesture.OPEN_PALM, Gesture.UNKNOWN, Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 0.81),
        (Gesture.OPEN_PALM, Gesture.FIST, Gesture.UNKNOWN, 0.0),
    ],
)
def test_confidence_matches_the_selected_classifier(
    monkeypatch,
    smoothed,
    raw,
    expected,
    confidence,
):
    pipeline = AnalysisPipeline()
    results = iter(
        [
            SimpleNamespace(gesture=smoothed, confidence=0.81),
            SimpleNamespace(gesture=raw, confidence=0.85),
        ]
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(pipeline.gestures.classifier, "classify", lambda *args: next(results))
    result = pipeline.process(frame(sample(), 0), 100, 100)
    assert result.gesture == expected and result.confidence == confidence


def test_diagnostics_refresh_after_tracking_becomes_stale():
    runtime, clock = url_runtime()
    hold(runtime, clock)
    assert runtime.diagnostics()["effective_action"] == "browser.open_url"
    clock.value = 1500
    runtime.observe(analysis(800, Gesture.OPEN_PALM))
    assert runtime.diagnostics()["reason"] == "stale_tracking"
    assert runtime.diagnostics()["tracking_reason"] == "stale_tracking"
    assert len(runtime.queue) == 0


def test_diagnostics_refresh_after_hand_is_lost():
    runtime, clock = url_runtime()
    hold(runtime, clock)
    clock.value = 800
    runtime.observe(analysis(800, Gesture.NONE, Quality.NO_HAND))
    diag = runtime.diagnostics()
    assert diag["gesture"] == "NONE"
    assert diag["tracking_reason"] == "tracking_quality_no_hand"
    assert len(runtime.queue) == 0


def test_switching_live_revokes_already_queued_dry_run():
    runtime, clock = url_runtime(dry_run=True)
    hold(runtime, clock)
    assert len(runtime.queue) == 1
    runtime.set_dry_run(False)
    assert runtime.safety.paused and len(runtime.queue) == 0
    assert not runtime.executor.dry_run
    assert not runtime.safety.config["execution"]["dry_run"]
    assert runtime.process_one() is None
    runtime.resume()
    hold(runtime, clock, 800, 1400)
    assert runtime.process_one() is None
    clock.value = 1500
    runtime.observe(analysis(1500, Gesture.OPEN_PALM))
    assert len(runtime.queue) == 1


def test_only_open_url_is_exempt_from_foreground_target():
    assert {name for name, spec in ACTION_CATALOG.items() if not spec.requires_target} == {
        "browser.open_url",
    }


def test_url_failure_is_reported_and_does_not_stick_busy(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_actions.webbrowser, "open", lambda *a, **k: False)
    runtime, clock = url_runtime()
    hold(runtime, clock)
    result = runtime.process_one()
    assert not result.executed and result.reason.startswith("handler_error:")
    assert runtime.safety._active is None


def test_saved_live_config_still_requires_explicit_resume(monkeypatch):
    config = policy("browser.open_url")
    config["mode"] = "Browser"
    config["execution"]["dry_run"] = False
    config["safety"]["paused"] = True
    config["bindings"] = {"OPEN_PALM": "browser.open_url"}
    config["targets"]["urls"]["browser.open_url"] = "https://example.com"
    clock = Clock()
    own = Foreground(71, os.getpid(), "C:/Apps/python.exe")
    runtime = ActionRuntime(config, ContextDetector(lambda: own, clock), clock)
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_actions.webbrowser, "open", lambda *a, **k: calls.append(a) or True)
    hold(runtime, clock)
    assert runtime.process_one() is None
    runtime.resume()
    hold(runtime, clock, 800, 1500)
    assert runtime.process_one().executed
    expected = config["targets"]["urls"]["browser.open_url"]
    assert calls == [(expected,)]

========== PLIK: tests\test_pose_provider.py ==========
import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.domain.enums import QualityStatus, TrackingStatus
from src.gesture_assistant.domain.models import Landmark, PoseFrame
from src.gesture_assistant.vision.pose_engine import ModelNotFoundError, PoseEngine
from src.gesture_assistant.vision.pose_provider import FakePoseProvider
from src.gesture_assistant.vision.quality import evaluate_quality


def test_fake_provider_returns_frame_with_timestamp() -> None:
    provider = FakePoseProvider()
    frame = np.zeros((240, 320, 3), dtype=np.uint8)

    result = provider.process(frame, 1234)

    assert result.frame.timestamp_ms == 1234
    assert result.frame.image_width == 320
    assert result.frame.image_height == 240
    assert result.frame.tracking_status == TrackingStatus.TRACKING
    assert result.quality == QualityStatus.VALID


def test_fake_provider_increases_frame_id() -> None:
    provider = FakePoseProvider()
    frame = np.zeros((240, 320, 3), dtype=np.uint8)

    first = provider.process(frame, 1)
    second = provider.process(frame, 2)

    assert second.frame.frame_id == first.frame.frame_id + 1


def test_pose_engine_without_model_raises_error(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = PoseEngine.create(tmp_path / "missing.task")
    frame = np.zeros((240, 320, 3), dtype=np.uint8)

    assert engine.has_model is False

    with pytest.raises(ModelNotFoundError):
        engine.process(frame, 1)


def test_quality_no_person() -> None:
    frame = PoseFrame(
        frame_id=1,
        timestamp_ms=1,
        image_width=320,
        image_height=240,
        landmarks=None,
        world_landmarks=None,
        tracking_status=TrackingStatus.NO_PERSON,
    )

    quality, message = evaluate_quality(frame)

    assert quality == QualityStatus.NO_PERSON
    assert message is not None


def test_quality_low_visibility() -> None:
    landmarks = [Landmark(x=0.5, y=0.5, z=0.0, visibility=0.1) for _ in range(33)]
    frame = PoseFrame(
        frame_id=1,
        timestamp_ms=1,
        image_width=320,
        image_height=240,
        landmarks=landmarks,
        world_landmarks=None,
        tracking_status=TrackingStatus.TRACKING,
    )

    quality, _ = evaluate_quality(frame)

    assert quality == QualityStatus.LOW_VISIBILITY


def test_quality_out_of_frame() -> None:
    landmarks = [Landmark(x=-0.5, y=0.5, z=0.0, visibility=0.9) for _ in range(33)]
    frame = PoseFrame(
        frame_id=1,
        timestamp_ms=1,
        image_width=320,
        image_height=240,
        landmarks=landmarks,
        world_landmarks=None,
        tracking_status=TrackingStatus.TRACKING,
    )

    quality, _ = evaluate_quality(frame)

    assert quality == QualityStatus.OUT_OF_FRAME

========== PLIK: tests\test_presentation_actions.py ==========
import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.config.schema import validate_config
from src.gesture_assistant.core.config_manager import defaults
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations import presentation_actions as presentation
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.dispatcher import NativeDispatcher
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.native_backend import AuthorizationLost
from tests.integration_samples import FakeKeyboard

CASES = [
    ("next_slide", "right"),
    ("previous_slide", "left"),
    ("start_presentation", "f5"),
    ("end_presentation", "escape"),
]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name, expected", CASES)
def test_default_presentation_shortcut(name, expected):
    keyboard = FakeKeyboard()
    getattr(presentation, name)(keyboard, lambda: True)
    assert keyboard.calls == [(expected, (expected,), False)]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name, expected", CASES)
def test_presentation_override(name, expected):
    keyboard = FakeKeyboard()
    action_id = f"presentation.{name}"
    getattr(presentation, name)(keyboard, lambda: True, {action_id: "ctrl+shift+p"})
    assert keyboard.calls[0][0] == "ctrl+shift+p"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name, expected", CASES)
def test_no_authorization_no_keyboard(name, expected):
    keyboard = FakeKeyboard()
    with pytest.raises(AuthorizationLost):
        getattr(presentation, name)(keyboard, lambda: False)
    assert not keyboard.calls


def test_presentation_error_propagates_to_executor_boundary():
    class BrokenKeyboard:
        def hotkey(self, *args, **kwargs):
            raise OSError("controlled input failure")

    with pytest.raises(OSError, match="controlled input failure"):
        presentation.next_slide(BrokenKeyboard(), lambda: True)


def test_config_accepts_presentation_shortcuts_and_rejects_destructive_override():
    config = defaults()
    config["targets"]["shortcuts"]["presentation.next_slide"] = "pagedown"
    assert validate_config(config)["targets"]["shortcuts"]["presentation.next_slide"] == "pagedown"
    config["targets"]["shortcuts"]["presentation.next_slide"] = "alt+f4"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        NativeDispatcher(config, FakeKeyboard())

========== PLIK: tests\test_runtime_lifecycle.py ==========
import threading

import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.core.action_executor import ActionExecutor
from src.gesture_assistant.core.action_runtime import ActionRuntime
from tests.action_samples import policy, ready


def test_consumer_starts_stops_and_can_restart_without_os_actions():
    runtime = ActionRuntime()
    for _ in range(2):
        try:
            runtime.start()
            assert runtime.is_running
            with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
                runtime.start()
        finally:
            runtime.stop()
            runtime._thread.join(timeout=2)
        assert not runtime.is_running
        assert runtime.safety.paused and len(runtime.queue) == 0


def test_a_different_media_action_cannot_start_during_active_handler():
    config = policy("media.play_pause")
    config["allowed_actions"].append("media.stop")
    safety, clock, detector = ready("media.play_pause", "vlc.exe", config)
    request = safety.request("media.play_pause", "FIST", detector.detect())
    entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
    outcomes = []

    def handler():
        entered.set()
        assert release.wait(2), "Nie zwolniono atrapy handlera."

    executor = ActionExecutor(safety, detector, {"media.play_pause": handler}, False)
    thread = threading.Thread(target=lambda: outcomes.append(executor.execute(request)))
    try:
        thread.start()
        assert entered.wait(2)
        assert safety.request("media.stop", "FIST", detector.detect()) is None
    finally:
        release.set()
        thread.join(timeout=2)
    assert not thread.is_alive()
    assert outcomes[0].executed

from dataclasses import replace

from src.gesture_assistant.core.action_executor import ActionExecutor
from src.gesture_assistant.core.action_queue import ActionQueue
from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.definitions import Gesture, Motion, Quality
from tests.action_samples import analysis, context, feed, policy, ready


def test_requires_real_700ms_not_five_fast_frames():
    safety, clock, detector = ready()
    safety.resume()
    feed(safety, clock, 800, 1200)
    assert safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect()) is None
    feed(safety, clock, 1300, 1500)
    assert safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect()) is not None


def test_pause_invalidates_pending_and_configuration_is_not_mutable():
    safety, clock, detector = ready()
    request = safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect())
    assert request is not None
    config = safety.config
    config["safety"]["paused"] = True
    assert not safety.paused
    safety.pause()
    assert safety.reserve(request, detector.detect()) == "invalid_or_consumed_request"
    assert safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect()) is None


def test_cooldown_release_replay_and_dryrun():
    safety, clock, detector = ready()
    request = safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect())
    executor = ActionExecutor(safety, detector)
    outcome = executor.execute(request)
    assert outcome.accepted and not outcome.executed
    assert executor.execute(request).reason == "invalid_or_consumed_request"
    feed(safety, clock, 800, 2800)
    assert safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect()) is None
    feed(safety, clock, 2900, 3900, Gesture.NONE, Quality.NO_HAND)
    feed(safety, clock, 4000, 4700)
    assert safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect()) is not None


def test_system_actions_blocked_in_teams_and_browser():
    for name in ("ms-teams.exe", "chrome.exe"):
        safety, clock, detector = ready("system.lock", name)

        request = safety.request(
            "system.lock",
            "FIST",
            detector.detect(),
        )

        assert request is None

    safety, clock, detector = ready("system.lock", "unknown.exe")

    request = safety.request(
        "system.lock",
        "FIST",
        detector.detect(),
    )

    assert request is not None
    assert request.action_id == "system.lock"

    safety, clock, detector = ready("system.lock", "explorer.exe")
    detector.reader = lambda: None

    request = safety.request(
        "system.lock",
        "FIST",
        detector.detect(),
    )

    assert request is None


def test_sleep_off_and_destructive_confirmation():
    config = policy("sleep_computer")

    safety, clock, detector = ready(
        "sleep_computer",
        "explorer.exe",
        config,
    )

    assert (
        safety.request(
            "sleep_computer",
            "FIST",
            detector.detect(),
        )
        is None
    )

    config["safety"]["sleep_computer"] = True

    safety, clock, detector = ready(
        "sleep_computer",
        "explorer.exe",
        config,
    )

    request = safety.request(
        "sleep_computer",
        "FIST",
        detector.detect(),
    )

    assert request is not None

    assert (
        safety.reserve(
            request,
            detector.detect(),
        )
        == "destructive_not_enabled_in_settings"
    )

    config["safety"]["confirmed_destructive"] = ["sleep_computer"]

    safety, clock, detector = ready(
        "sleep_computer",
        "explorer.exe",
        config,
    )

    request = safety.request(
        "sleep_computer",
        "FIST",
        detector.detect(),
    )

    assert request is not None

    assert (
        safety.reserve(
            request,
            detector.detect(),
        )
        == "explicit_confirmation_required"
    )

    safety.confirm_once(
        "sleep_computer",
        detector.detect(),
    )

    assert (
        safety.request(
            "sleep_computer",
            "FIST",
            detector.detect(),
        )
        is None
    )

    feed(safety, clock, 800, 1500)

    request = safety.request(
        "sleep_computer",
        "FIST",
        detector.detect(),
    )

    assert request is not None
    assert safety.reserve(request, detector.detect()) == ""

    safety.finish(request)


def test_context_revalidated_and_tracking_gap_resets_hold():

def test_motion_requires_open_palm_hold_and_cooldown():

    feed(safety, clock, 800, 1500, Gesture.OPEN_PALM)

def test_queue_bounded_and_deduplicated():
    safety, clock, detector = ready()
    request = safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect())
    queue = ActionQueue(1)
    assert queue.put(request)
    assert not queue.put(request)
    assert not queue.put(replace(request, token="different"))
    assert queue.pop() == request
    assert queue.pop() is None


def test_timestamp_replay_future_and_long_gap_cannot_fake_release():

    assert not safety.observe(analysis(900))

    clock.value = 5000
    safety.observe(analysis(5000, Gesture.NONE, Quality.NO_HAND))
    feed(safety, clock, 5100, 5800)
    assert safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "FIST", detector.detect()) is None

    feed(safety, clock, 1600, 2700, Gesture.OPEN_PALM)
    assert safety.request("teams.toggle_mute", "OPEN_PALM", detector.detect()) is not None
