========== PLIK: tests\test_smoothing.py ==========
import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.smoothing import LandmarkSmoother
from tests.gesture_samples import sample


def test_ema():
    s = LandmarkSmoother(alpha=0.5)
    a = s.update(sample(), 0)
    b = s.update(sample(dx=0.1), 10)
    assert b.landmarks[0].x == pytest.approx(a.landmarks[0].x + 0.05)


def test_loss_and_long_gap_reset():
    s = LandmarkSmoother(alpha=0.5)
    s.update(sample(), 0)
    assert s.update(None, 20) is None
    new = sample(dx=0.1)
    assert s.update(new, 30).landmarks == new.landmarks
    other = sample(dx=-0.1)
    assert s.update(other, 1000).landmarks == other.landmarks


def test_side_switch_resets():
    s = LandmarkSmoother()
    s.update(sample(side="Right"), 0)
    left = sample(side="Left")
    assert s.update(left, 10).landmarks == left.landmarks

========== KONIEC PLIKU ==========

========== PLIK: tests\test_swipe_and_actions_enhanced.py ==========
from copy import deepcopy

import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.core.action_runtime import ActionRuntime
from src.gesture_assistant.core.analysis_pipeline import AnalysisPipeline
from src.gesture_assistant.core.config_manager import ConfigManager, defaults
from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.classifier import GestureClassifier
from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.definitions import Gesture, Motion, Quality
from src.gesture_assistant.gestures.motion_classifier import MotionClassifier
from tests.action_samples import Clock, analysis, context, policy
from tests.gesture_samples import frame, sample


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. CZTERY KIERUNKI SWIPE
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "start, end, expected",
    [
        ((0.2, 0.5), (0.55, 0.5), Motion.SWIPE_RIGHT),
        ((0.7, 0.5), (0.35, 0.5), Motion.SWIPE_LEFT),
        ((0.5, 0.7), (0.5, 0.35), Motion.SWIPE_UP),
        ((0.5, 0.3), (0.5, 0.65), Motion.SWIPE_DOWN),
    ],
)
def test_swipes_all_four_directions(start, end, expected):
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    steps = 4
    res = Motion.NONE
    for i in range(steps):
        x = start[0] + (end[0] - start[0]) * (i / (steps - 1))
        y = start[1] + (end[1] - start[1]) * (i / (steps - 1))
        res = classifier.update((x, y), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, i * 60)
    assert res == expected


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. RUCH SZYBKI I WOLNIEJSZY W OBSŁUGIWANYM ZAKRESIE
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_swipe_fast_motion():
    """Szybki ruch (3 klatki, 60 ms, duży krok)."""
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    assert classifier.update((0.2, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 0) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.35, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 30) == Motion.NONE
    res = classifier.update((0.52, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 60)
    assert res == Motion.SWIPE_RIGHT


def test_swipe_slow_motion():
    """Wolniejszy ruch (10 klatek w ciągu 500 ms, poniżej limitu 700 ms)."""
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    res = Motion.NONE
    for i in range(10):
        x = 0.2 + (0.32 * (i / 9))
        res = classifier.update((x, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, i * 50)
    assert res == Motion.SWIPE_RIGHT


def test_swipe_exceeding_max_duration_is_rejected():
    """Ruch zbyt powolny (przekraczający 700 ms)."""
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    res = Motion.NONE
    for i in range(10):
        x = 0.2 + (0.32 * (i / 9))
        res = classifier.update((x, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, i * 100)  # 0 do 900 ms
    assert res == Motion.NONE


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. ZMIENNY FPS (15 FPS, 30 FPS, 60 FPS)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
@pytest.mark.parametrize("fps, interval_ms", [(15, 66), (30, 33), (60, 16)])
def test_swipe_variable_fps(fps, interval_ms):
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    total_time_ms = 300
    n_frames = max(4, total_time_ms // interval_ms)
    res = Motion.NONE
    for i in range(n_frames):
        x = 0.2 + 0.3 * (i / (n_frames - 1))
        t = i * interval_ms
        res = classifier.update((x, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, t)
    assert res == Motion.SWIPE_RIGHT


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. KRÓTKIE UNKNOWN PODCZAS POPRAWNEJ TRAJEKTORII
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_swipe_tolerates_brief_unknown_during_trajectory():
    """Chwilowe zaburzenie (UNKNOWN) nie kasuje trajektorii, jeśli dłoń pozostaje stabilna."""
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    assert classifier.update((0.20, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 0) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.28, 0.5), Gesture.UNKNOWN, 50) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.38, 0.5), Gesture.UNKNOWN, 100) == Motion.NONE
    res = classifier.update((0.52, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 150)
    assert res == Motion.SWIPE_RIGHT


def test_swipe_without_any_open_palm_is_rejected():
    """Ruch wykonany wyłącznie z gestem UNKNOWN nie jest klasyfikowany jako swipe."""
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    for i in range(5):
        x = 0.2 + 0.3 * (i / 4)
        assert classifier.update((x, 0.5), Gesture.UNKNOWN, i * 50) == Motion.NONE


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. DRŻENIE BEZ SWIPE
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_jitter_without_swipe():
    """Drobne drgania w miejscu nie wywołują swipe."""
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    jitters = [(0.50, 0.50), (0.52, 0.51), (0.49, 0.49), (0.51, 0.50), (0.50, 0.51)]
    for i, pt in enumerate(jitters):
        assert classifier.update(pt, Gesture.OPEN_PALM, i * 50) == Motion.NONE


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. UTRATA DŁONI, ZMIANA DŁONI I SKOK POZYCJI
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_hand_loss_resets_motion():
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    assert classifier.update((0.2, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 0) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.3, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 50) == Motion.NONE
    # Utrata dłoni
    assert classifier.update(None, Gesture.NONE, 100) == Motion.NONE
    # Nowa dłoń musi zacząć od nowa
    assert classifier.update((0.4, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 150) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.5, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 200) == Motion.NONE


def test_position_jump_teleport_resets_motion():
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    assert classifier.update((0.1, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 0) == Motion.NONE
    # Skok o 0.55 w 50 ms (ponad max_step = 0.35)
    assert classifier.update((0.65, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 50) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.75, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 100) == Motion.NONE


def test_conflicting_static_gesture_resets_motion():
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    assert classifier.update((0.2, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 0) == Motion.NONE
    # Wyraźny gest pięści w trakcie ruchu
    assert classifier.update((0.35, 0.5), Gesture.FIST, 50) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.55, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 100) == Motion.NONE


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. BRAK WIELOKROTNEGO WYKONANIA I KOLEJNE ŚWIADOME SWIPE
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_single_swipe_produces_single_event_and_subsequent_swipe_after_cooldown():
    classifier = MotionClassifier()
    # Pierwszy swipe
    assert classifier.update((0.2, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 0) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.3, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 50) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.4, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 100) == Motion.NONE
    res1 = classifier.update((0.52, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 150)
    assert res1 == Motion.SWIPE_RIGHT

    # Kolejna klatka natychmiast po swipe NIE może wywołać kolejnego zdarzenia
    assert classifier.update((0.55, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 200) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.60, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 300) == Motion.NONE

    # Próba nowego swipe przed upływem cooldown (1500 ms)
    for i in range(4):
        assert (
            classifier.update((0.2 + i * 0.1, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, 500 + i * 50) == Motion.NONE
        )

    # Po upływie cooldownu (t > 150 + 1500 = 1650 ms), kolejny świadomy swipe DZIAŁA
    t_base = 1800
    assert classifier.update((0.2, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, t_base) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.3, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, t_base + 50) == Motion.NONE
    assert classifier.update((0.4, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, t_base + 100) == Motion.NONE
    res2 = classifier.update((0.52, 0.5), Gesture.OPEN_PALM, t_base + 150)
    assert res2 == Motion.SWIPE_RIGHT


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. PRZEPŁYW OD PIPELINE DO HANDLERA Z ATRAPĄ BACKENDU
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_full_pipeline_to_handler_swipe():
    """Zdarzenie swipe z AnalysisPipeline przechodzi autoryzację i trafia do handlera."""
    clock = Clock()
    cfg = policy("presentation.next_slide")
    cfg["profiles"]["Presentation"]["SWIPE_RIGHT"] = "presentation.next_slide"
    cfg["allowed_actions"] = ["presentation.next_slide"]
    cfg["execution"]["dry_run"] = False

    det = context(clock, "powerpnt.exe")
    runtime = ActionRuntime(cfg, det, clock)
    calls = []
    runtime.executor.handlers = {"presentation.next_slide": lambda: calls.append("next")}
    runtime.executor.dry_run = False
    runtime.resume()

    # Generujemy klatki swipe z otwartą dłonią
    pipeline = AnalysisPipeline()
    for i in range(4):
        t = i * 100
        clock.value = t
        h = sample(Gesture.OPEN_PALM, scale=0.5, dx=-0.2 + i * 0.1)
        res = pipeline.process(frame(h, t), 100, 100)
        runtime.observe(res)

    # W 4. klatce pipeline wykrywa SWIPE_RIGHT
    assert len(runtime.queue) == 1
    outcome = runtime.process_one()
    assert outcome is not None
    assert outcome.executed
    assert outcome.action_id == "presentation.next_slide"
    assert calls == ["next"]


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. ZACHOWANIE PAUSED, DRY-RUN, LIVE I ALLOWED_ACTIONS
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_paused_blocks_execution():
    clock = Clock()
    cfg = policy("teams.toggle_mute")
    cfg["safety"]["paused"] = True
    runtime = ActionRuntime(cfg, context(clock), clock)
    assert runtime.safety.paused

    # Gest przytrzymany 700 ms, ale system jest PAUSED
    for t in range(0, 701, 100):
        clock.value = t
        runtime.observe(analysis(t, Gesture.FIST))
    assert len(runtime.queue) == 0
    assert runtime.process_one() is None


def test_dry_run_accepts_without_executing_handler():
    clock = Clock()
    cfg = policy("teams.toggle_mute")
    cfg["execution"]["dry_run"] = True
    runtime = ActionRuntime(cfg, context(clock), clock)
    runtime.resume()
    calls = []
    runtime.executor.handlers = {"teams.toggle_mute": lambda: calls.append(1)}

    for t in range(0, 701, 100):
        clock.value = t
        runtime.observe(analysis(t, Gesture.FIST))

    assert len(runtime.queue) == 1
    outcome = runtime.process_one()
    assert outcome is not None
    assert outcome.accepted
    assert not outcome.executed
    assert outcome.reason == "dry_run_no_side_effect"
    assert not calls


def test_live_executes_handler():
    clock = Clock()
    cfg = policy("teams.toggle_mute")
    cfg["execution"]["dry_run"] = False
    runtime = ActionRuntime(cfg, context(clock), clock)
    runtime.executor.dry_run = False
    runtime.resume()
    calls = []
    runtime.executor.handlers = {"teams.toggle_mute": lambda: calls.append(1)}

    for t in range(0, 701, 100):
        clock.value = t
        runtime.observe(analysis(t, Gesture.FIST))

    assert len(runtime.queue) == 1
    outcome = runtime.process_one()
    assert outcome is not None
    assert outcome.executed
    assert calls == [1]


def test_not_allowed_action_blocks_request():
    clock = Clock()
    cfg = policy("teams.toggle_mute")
    cfg["allowed_actions"] = []  # Pusta lista dozwolonych
    runtime = ActionRuntime(cfg, context(clock), clock)
    runtime.resume()

    for t in range(0, 701, 100):
        clock.value = t
        runtime.observe(analysis(t, Gesture.FIST))

    assert len(runtime.queue) == 0


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. POPRAWNOŚĆ MAPOWAŃ, NADPISAŃ I „BRAK AKCJI”
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_global_bindings_override_profile_and_none_action_does_not_inherit():
    clock = Clock()
    cfg = defaults()
    cfg["safety"]["enabled"] = True
    cfg["safety"]["paused"] = False

    # Profil Media ma domyślnie FIST -> media.play_pause
    assert cfg["profiles"]["Media"]["FIST"] == "media.play_pause"

    # Nadpisanie globalne FIST -> system.toggle_mute
    cfg["bindings"]["FIST"] = "system.toggle_mute"
    cfg["allowed_actions"] = ["system.toggle_mute", "media.play_pause"]

    runtime = ActionRuntime(cfg, context(clock, "vlc.exe"), clock)
    runtime.resume()

    # Obserwacja gestu FIST w profilu Media: powinno wybrać globalne system.toggle_mute
    for t in range(0, 701, 100):
        clock.value = t
        runtime.observe(analysis(t, Gesture.FIST))

    # Sprawdzamy efektywne przypisanie z reguły globalnej w runtime.diagnostics()
    diag = runtime.diagnostics()
    assert diag["effective_action"] == "system.toggle_mute"
    assert diag["action_source"] == "Globalne"

    # Teraz ustawiamy "Brak akcji" w bindings dla FIST
    cfg["bindings"]["FIST"] = "none"
    runtime.apply_config(cfg)
    runtime.resume()

    for t in range(800, 1501, 100):
        clock.value = t
        runtime.observe(analysis(t, Gesture.FIST))

    diag2 = runtime.diagnostics()
    assert diag2["effective_action"] is None
    assert diag2["action_source"] == "Brak akcji (Globalne)"
    assert len(runtime.queue) == 0


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 11. ZAPIS, ODCZYT I ZASTOSOWANIE NOWEJ KONFIGURACJI ORAZ UNIEWAŻNIENIE OCZEKUJĄCYCH
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_config_save_load_and_dynamic_apply(tmp_path):
    cfg_file = tmp_path / "test_config.json"
    manager = ConfigManager(cfg_file)
    cfg = defaults()
    cfg["thresholds"]["swipe_distance"] = 0.20
    cfg["thresholds"]["gesture_hold_ms"] = 800
    cfg["bindings"]["SWIPE_UP"] = "system.lock"
    manager.save(cfg)

    loaded = manager.load().config
    assert loaded["thresholds"]["swipe_distance"] == 0.20
    assert loaded["thresholds"]["gesture_hold_ms"] == 800
    assert loaded["bindings"]["SWIPE_UP"] == "system.lock"

    # Dynamiczne zastosowanie w runtime
    clock = Clock()
    runtime = ActionRuntime(defaults(), context(clock), clock)
    runtime.apply_config(loaded)
    assert runtime.safety.config["thresholds"]["gesture_hold_ms"] == 800
    assert runtime.safety.paused  # Zawsze wstrzymane po zastosowaniu zmian


def test_apply_config_invalidates_pending_requests():
    clock = Clock()
    cfg = policy("teams.toggle_mute")
    runtime = ActionRuntime(cfg, context(clock), clock)
    runtime.resume()

    # Tworzymy żądanie w kolejce
    for t in range(0, 701, 100):
        clock.value = t
        runtime.observe(analysis(t, Gesture.FIST))
    assert len(runtime.queue) == 1

    # Podmiana konfiguracji w trakcie oczekiwania żądania w kolejce
    new_cfg = deepcopy(cfg)
    new_cfg["thresholds"]["gesture_hold_ms"] = 900
    runtime.apply_config(new_cfg)

    # Kolejka musi zostać wyczyszczona
    assert len(runtime.queue) == 0
    assert runtime.process_one() is None


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 12. REGRESJA ISTNIEJĄCYCH GESTÓW STATYCZNYCH
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "gesture",
    [
        Gesture.OPEN_PALM,
        Gesture.FIST,
        Gesture.THUMBS_UP,
        Gesture.THUMBS_DOWN,
        Gesture.VICTORY,
        Gesture.POINTING_UP,
        Gesture.PINCH,
        Gesture.ROCK,
    ],
)
def test_static_gestures_classification_and_confidence(gesture):
    classifier = GestureClassifier()
    hand_sample = sample(gesture)
    res = classifier.classify(hand_sample)
    assert res.gesture == gesture
    assert res.quality == Quality.VALID
    assert 0.70 <= res.confidence <= 0.85

========== PLIK: tests\test_teams.py ==========
import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.integrations import teams
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.contracts import IntegrationNotImplemented


def test_teams_catalog_and_legacy_entrypoint():
    expected_actions = {
        "teams.toggle_mute",
        "teams.toggle_raise_hand",
        "teams.toggle_camera",
        "teams.reaction_like",
        "teams.reaction_applause",
        "teams.leave_meeting",
    }

    actions = {action.action_id: action for action in teams.ACTIONS}

    assert set(actions) == expected_actions
    assert all(action.implemented for action in actions.values())
    assert actions["teams.leave_meeting"].destructive

    functions = (
        teams.toggle_mute,
        teams.toggle_raise_hand,
        teams.toggle_camera,
        teams.reaction_like,
        teams.reaction_applause,
        teams.leave_meeting,
    )

    assert all(callable(function) for function in functions)

    with pytest.raises(IntegrationNotImplemented):
        teams.execute()

========== PLIK: tests\test_ui_stage2.py ==========
from PySide6.QtCore import QSize

from src.gesture_assistant.ui.camera_view import CameraView
from src.gesture_assistant.ui.main_window import MainWindow


def test_camera_view_preserves_aspect(qapp):
    view = CameraView()
    view.resize(1000, 500)
    rect = view._scaled_target_rect(QSize(640, 480))
    assert abs(rect.width() / rect.height() - 640 / 480) < 0.01
    assert rect.width() <= view.width()
    assert rect.height() <= view.height()
    view.close()


def test_diagnostic_panel_constructs_without_camera(qapp, tmp_path):
    window = MainWindow(config_path=tmp_path / "gui.json")
    assert window._worker is None
    assert "Gest" in window._diagnostic_label.text()
    assert window._start_button.isEnabled()
    window.close()


def test_actions_start_paused_and_no_executor_is_running(qapp, tmp_path):
    window = MainWindow(config_path=tmp_path / "gui.json")

    try:
        assert window._worker is None
        assert window._runtime.safety.paused
        assert window._runtime.executor.dry_run
        assert not window._runtime.executor.handlers
        assert not window._runtime.is_running

        window._toggle_actions_pause()

        assert window._runtime.safety.paused
        assert not window._runtime.is_running
        assert "Najpierw uruchom kamerę" in window._error_label.text()

        window._stop_analysis()

        assert window._runtime.safety.paused

    finally:
        window.close()


def test_saving_global_url_keeps_other_gestures_inheriting(qapp, tmp_path):
    window = MainWindow(config_path=tmp_path / "gui.json")
    try:
        palm = window._signal_row_widgets["OPEN_PALM"]
        assert palm["combo"].currentData() == "inherit"
        palm["combo"].setCurrentIndex(palm["combo"].findData("browser.open_url"))
        palm["allowed_check"].setChecked(True)
        palm["url_edit"].setText("https://example.com")
        window._save_bindings_from_ui()
        assert window._config["bindings"] == {"OPEN_PALM": "browser.open_url"}
        assert "browser.open_url" in window._config["allowed_actions"]
        assert window._runtime.safety.paused
        palm = window._signal_row_widgets["OPEN_PALM"]
        palm["combo"].setCurrentIndex(palm["combo"].findData("inherit"))
        window._save_bindings_from_ui()
        assert "OPEN_PALM" not in window._config["bindings"]
    finally:
        window.close()


def test_live_button_keeps_checkbox_and_runtime_in_sync(qapp, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from PySide6.QtWidgets import QMessageBox

    window = MainWindow(config_path=tmp_path / "gui.json")
    monkeypatch.setattr(QMessageBox, "warning", lambda *a, **k: QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes)
    try:
        window._toggle_execution_mode()
        assert not window._config["execution"]["dry_run"]
        assert not window._dry_run_check.isChecked()
        assert not window._runtime.executor.dry_run
        assert not window._runtime.safety.config["execution"]["dry_run"]
        assert window._runtime.safety.paused
        window._save_settings_from_ui()
        assert not window._runtime.executor.dry_run
        window._toggle_execution_mode()
        assert window._dry_run_check.isChecked()
        assert window._runtime.executor.dry_run and window._runtime.safety.paused
    finally:
        window.close()

========== PLIK: tests\test_windows_actions.py ==========
import pytest

from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.keyboard_actions import validate_shortcut
from src.gesture_assistant.integrations.windows_actions import validate_application_path


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", ["", "relative.exe", "C:/missing.exe", "app.exe --flag", 12])
def test_bad_application_path(path):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_application_path(path)


def test_existing_executable_path(tmp_path):
    exe = tmp_path / "test.exe"
    exe.write_bytes(b"not executed")
    assert validate_application_path(str(exe)) == exe.resolve()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("shortcut", ["ctrl++a", "ctrl+ctrl+a", "ctrl", "a+b", "win+l", "", 1])
def test_bad_shortcut(shortcut):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_shortcut(shortcut)


def test_good_shortcut():
    assert validate_shortcut("Ctrl + Shift + M") == ("ctrl", "shift", "m")

========== PLIK: tests\test_workspace.py ==========
from src.gesture_assistant.ui.main_window import MainWindow


def test_workspace_navigation_and_empty_history(qapp, tmp_path):
    window = MainWindow(tmp_path / "config.json")
    try:
        workspace = window._workspace
        for index, button in enumerate(workspace.nav):
            button.click()
            assert workspace.pages.currentIndex() == index
            assert button.isChecked()
        workspace.navigate(2)
        assert workspace.rows.count() == 0
        assert not workspace.export_button.isEnabled()
    finally:
        window.close()


def test_error_preserves_camera_failure_message(qapp, tmp_path):
    window = MainWindow(tmp_path / "config.json")
    try:
        window._on_error("Nie można otworzyć kamery.")
        assert window._runtime.safety.paused
        assert "Błąd kamery" in window._status_label.text()
        assert "Nie można otworzyć" in window._error_label.text()
    finally:
        window.close()
